# Agents · A · part 9

937 entries, agentworks-tester to ai-ml-engineer-nlp. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agentworks-tester**: Exercises the real agentworks CLI against live backends and reports findings from observed behavior. Invoke with a scoped charter, an environment inventory, and a resource budget. Does not fix anything; produces an evidence-backed report and leaves no residue. ([WayfarerLabs/agentworks](https://github.com/WayfarerLabs/agentworks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agentworks-tester.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **agenty**: Drive ComfyUI, partner (API) models, 3D apps (Blender, Maya, Houdini), World Labs Atlas and the Deadline farm with agentY's tools. Use for any image, video or 3D job that needs more than one or two tool calls, so the long tool traffic stays out of the main conversation; hand it the request and every constraint (model, size, count, references) and it returns the output paths and a verdict. ([szprivate/agentY-mcp](https://github.com/szprivate/agentY-mcp/blob/HEAD/agents/agenty.md))
- **agenty-scout**: Cheap, read-only look-ups about the system: which templates, models, nodes or skills exist, what a workflow does, which model suits a task, what a node's inputs are, or facts and reference images from the web. Never builds or runs anything. Use before deciding, or for a question that only needs an answer. ([szprivate/agentY-mcp](https://github.com/szprivate/agentY-mcp/blob/HEAD/agents/agenty-scout.md))
- **agentyx-reviewer**: Reviews changes for correctness, security, regressions, and missing tests. ([Andersseen/agentyx](https://github.com/Andersseen/agentyx/blob/HEAD/packages/cli/test/fixtures/lock-v1/.claude/agents/agentyx-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **ager-translator**: Compiles a validated AGER bundle into a Claude Code plugin. Does not author graphs. ([SpillwaveSolutions/claude-code-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/claude-code-ager/blob/HEAD/agents/ager-translator.md))
- **ageyo-lead**: AGEYO（旧 Givers）専用の部署長。ディレクターから「AGEYOを進めて」と言われたら立てる。正本パス・ビルド手順・AGEYO固有の地雷を最初から知った状態で、自分では実装せず Agent の並列起動で worker / verifier を編成し担当領域が終わるまで自走する。 ([hikaru777/SettingOfClaudeCode](https://github.com/hikaru777/SettingOfClaudeCode/blob/HEAD/agents/ageyo-lead.md))

- **aggregating-test-reviewer**: Type-aware end-to-end verification gate. For frontend / full-stack stages runs the FULL review (boots dev server, runs CI gates locally, drives Claude-in-Chrome user-acceptance tests against the slice's user-facing surfaces, compares visual diffs against design-system tokens). For backend / db-schema / infrastructure stages runs the REDUCED review (CI gates only, no browser UAT, no visual diff). For design-system / ci-cd / env-setup stages this agent is SKIPPED by the orchestrator. Dispatched by sell-slice Phase 7. ([steve-piece/bytheslice](https://github.com/steve-piece/bytheslice/blob/HEAD/skills/sell-slice/agents/aggregating-test-reviewer.md))
- **aggregation-expert**: Use this agent for MongoDB aggregation pipelines, $lookup joins, $merge and $out for materialized views, $facet for multi-dimensional analysis, pipeline optimization, window functions, bucket operations, $graphLookup for graph traversals, and all aggregation expression and accumulator operators. - **aggregator**: Merges several reviews of one target into the one report a gate reads, tagging each finding with its consensus and computing the verdict from the thresholds it is handed; also synthesises research findings across iterations. Hand over `REVIEWS:` (one path per line), `THRESHOLDS:` quoted verbatim and `OUTPUT:`, or `SESSION_PATH:` plus `ITERATION:` for research. It never opens the code the reviews judged. Use when two or more reviews of one target need one file; a single review needs no aggregator. ([MadAppGang/magus](https://github.com/MadAppGang/magus/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/agents/aggregator.md) · ★14)

- **agnes-builder**: Disciplined Agnes feature implementer. Use when adding a data-source connector, REST API endpoint, web page, repository (method), or schema migration. - **agnes-e2e-tester**: Use when verifying Agnes end-to-end — running/triaging the E2E suites (web chat, Slack, E2B, MCP), smoke-testing a feature before release, or walking the manual acceptance checklist against a live instance. Knows the env-var gates, the docker-compose harness, cost guardrails, and the per-surface verification checklists. Verifies and reports — it does not implement features (use agnes-builder for that). - **agnes-integrator**: Integrates the build team's parallel worktree results into one coherent change — applies tasks in dependency order, serializes the single migration task last, folds CHANGELOG/CLAUDE.md bullets in one pass, and stops on any unresolvable conflict. - **agnes-releaser**: Use before merging a PR (phase 1 — prepare release-cut commit) and after merge (phase 2 — tag + GitHub Release). Invoked explicitly by the user; never auto-fires. - **agnes-reviewer-parity**: Use when a PR diff touches src/repositories/*, src/db.py, migrations/, or tests/db_pg/. - **agrario**: Use este agente para Direito Agrário brasileiro na perspectiva do produtor rural e do agronegócio: contratos agrários (arrendamento, parceria, barter, integração, safra), crédito rural e dívida (CPR, cédulas, execução e embargos, revisional, prorrogação, MP 1.376/2026), RJ do produtor, terras e registral (due diligence, georreferenciamento, usucapião rural, estrangeiros, ITR), desapropriação e reforma agrária, ambiental do agro (CAR, PRA, embargo do IBAMA, licenciamento, EUDR) e possessório rural. SEM penal (crimes ambientais, grilagem) e SEM ACP ambiental — gap declarado. Ative em: "fazenda"… ([sbroggioadv/agrario-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/agrario-adv-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/agrario-adv-os/agents/agrario.md))
- **agrarrecht-drafter**: Du erstellst den **Entwurf** auf Basis der Quellen, die der Researcher geliefert hat. Du argumentierst, subsumierst, ziehst Schlussfolgerungen. Du gibst aber **keine** Mandatsentscheidung – die obliegt dem zugelassenen Rechtsanwalt. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/agrarrecht/agents/drafter.md) · ★61)
- **agrarrecht-researcher**: Du bist die **Recherche-Stufe** in der Researcher → Drafter → Reviewer-Pipeline. Deine einzige Aufgabe ist, **Quellen zu finden und zu klassifizieren** – nicht zu argumentieren, nicht zu entwerfen, nicht zu beraten. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/agrarrecht/agents/researcher.md) · ★61)
- **agrarrecht-reviewer**: Du bist die **Qualitäts- und Risikostufe** vor Auslieferung an den Mandantenanwalt oder die zuständige Behörde / das Landwirtschaftsgericht. Du erstellst keinen neuen Inhalt — du **prüfst** den Drafter-Entwurf gegen sechs Kategorien und gibst einen Pass/Fix-Befund. ([borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law](https://github.com/borghei/AI-Skills-German-Law/blob/HEAD/agrarrecht/agents/reviewer.md) · ★61)
- **agreement-pass**: Convenes the Fable 5 + Sol expert-consensus seat the product law names as the pipeline-wide default at any point that would otherwise ask the operator. Spawn when the blocker ladder reaches it (two provably-distinct fix attempts have both failed) or at any other agreement-pass trigger the product law or the architecture phase names. Never spawn it to answer a question a worker could resolve alone, and never spawn it in place of asking the operator when the ambiguity is about operator intent — that goes to the operator directly, not here. ([Fredasterehub/kiln](https://github.com/Fredasterehub/kiln/blob/HEAD/plugins/kiln/agents/agreement-pass.md) · ★227)
- **agreements**: Per-module semantics for `@happyvertical/smrt-sales/agreements`. Package orientation, the cross-module invariants (currency, tenancy, cross-package refs, roles vs. money), and the Gotchas that apply before editing anything live in [../AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md) — read that first. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/sales/agents/agreements.md) · ★7)

Заменяет CONTENT_ARCHITECT + CONTENT_WRITER (и мандат так и не созданного CREATIVE_DIRECTOR). Use when producing or editing any content piece for the calendar. Заменяет KNOWLEDGE_MANAGER + COMPETITOR_ANALYST + LEAD_ANALYST. Use when a fact needs sourcing/verification, or competitor/lead data needs updating. - **agt-architecture**: Technical design agent for this repository. Transforms approved requirements.md into docs/specs/&lt;feature-slug&gt;/design.md, mapping layers, contracts, persistence, messaging, compatibility, idempotency, observability, rollout and rollback. Writes only under docs/specs/. Never edits src/ and never redefines product rules silently. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-architecture-analyst**: Consolidates docs/architecture/profile.md and patterns.md into a single narrative docs/architecture/analysis.md. Dispatches probe/miner when those sources are missing (unless SKIPPED_LAYERED_KIT). Does not edit application source or kit rules. Use after Path D profile/patterns or when the user asks for one complete architecture analysis markdown. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture-analyst.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-architecture-probe**: Architecture-agnostic probe. Inventories a repository as-is, classifies style without dogma (layered, hexagonal, MVC, hybrid, etc.), maps observed boundaries and dependency directions, and writes docs/architecture/profile.md. Does not edit application source or Cursor rules. Coexists with agt-architecture (SDD design) and agt-architecture-review (layered audit). ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture-probe.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-architecture-review**: Architecture audit for Node.js/TypeScript layered backends (Domain / Application / Infraestructure / Configuration). - **agt-code-quality**: Audits semantic naming, REST conventions, and light layer adherence in layered backend services. Never edits code. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-code-review.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **agt-product-owner**: Product Owner for this repository. Defines product outcomes, actors, business rules, flows, acceptance criteria, non-functional expectations, risks, assumptions, metrics, and out-of-scope. - **agy**: Dispatcher for the agy (Gemini) CLI backend — a configurable, equal tool, not a fixed task bucket. WHERE work routes is decided by config/roster.json (routes + tags.txt) and per-subtask /team assignments, not hardcoded here. By the shipped routing config, agy is the default lane for fast, commodity, easily-verifiable work and Gemini's edges: new UI/React components, CSS, SVG/animation, scaffolding, CRUD, REST endpoints, scripts, CLI tools, glue code, SQL, regex, configs, Dockerfiles, unit tests, fixtures, data transforms, web-search/doc summarization, bulk text, and audio/video (Gemini-exclus… ([WatchAndyTW/multi-model-team](https://github.com/WatchAndyTW/multi-model-team/blob/HEAD/agents/agy.md) · ★6)
- **agy**: Delegate one scoped task to the Antigravity CLI (agy) - implement, test, refactor, investigate, or review through agy or Gemini-family models - and return the evidence needed to judge it. Use when asked to delegate/offload to agy, Antigravity, or Gemini, and especially when several such runs should proceed at once. Dispatches via the agy-dispatch wrapper; returns evidence, never a verdict. ([jsavyasachi/ai-dotfiles](https://github.com/jsavyasachi/ai-dotfiles/blob/HEAD/extensions/agents/agy.md) · ★2)
- **agy**: Delegate a task to the `agy` CLI and report back what it produced. Use when the user asks to run something with agy, or wants a task handled by a non-Claude model such as Gemini or GPT-OSS. BEFORE dispatching, if the user did not name a model, run `agy models` and ask them to pick one with AskUserQuestion. List the CLI's configured default model first, labelled `(default)`; if there is no default, list the models in the order the CLI prints them. Pass the chosen model in the task prompt. ONLY use this agent when the user explicitly asks for it by name. Never invoke it on your own initiative o… ([squall-chua/skills](https://github.com/squall-chua/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/agy.md) · ★2)
- **agy**: `agy` is not a smarter model. It is a **separate quota**. The only reason to use it is context compression: it reads the files, and what comes back into Claude's context is twenty lines instead of twenty files. If the answer is inherently long, or you will re-read the code to trust it, delegating costs more than doing it yourself. ([jalejandrov93/agent-hub](https://github.com/jalejandrov93/agent-hub/blob/HEAD/skills/multi-agent-orchestrator/references/agents/agy.md) · ★1)
- **agy**: Delegate complex execution, deep reasoning, architectural planning, adversarial code review, session documentation, or web research to Google Antigravity CLI (agy). Use this subagent for pair programming, TDD implementation, second opinions, session summaries, and multi-turn collaboration. ([KZvilla/lagrange-agent-runtime](https://github.com/KZvilla/lagrange-agent-runtime/blob/HEAD/agents/agy.md) · ★1)

- **agy**: Runs exactly one agy-call command (a Gemini task, review, status, result or cancel) and returns its output unchanged. Use only when the user asks for Gemini or agy, by slash command or in plain words; never on your own initiative. ([shitanshuuppal/agy-call](https://github.com/shitanshuuppal/agy-call/blob/HEAD/agents/agy.md))
- **agy-agent**: Use this agent for deep codebase exploration when a task benefits from Antigravity CLI's large context window or from synthesizing many files in one pass. - **agy-agent**: General-purpose agent that hands a task to Google Antigravity (agy, Gemini) instead of doing it with Claude. On the default Gemini 3.8 Flash it is a fast cheap second pair of hands for quick investigations, research, mechanical edits and scripted chores. For a code review or a general task handoff that needs judgement, start the brief with `model: gemini-4-argon-medium` (Gemini 4 Argon; use `-high` only when the user asks for it), and say read-only for a review. Argon is slow: a brief that bundles exploration, reasoning, fixes and tests tends to run past the 30-minute default timeout and come… ([Yuripetusko/skills](https://github.com/Yuripetusko/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/agy-delegate/agents/agy-agent.md))
- **agy-argon**: Gemini 4 Argon via the Antigravity CLI. For fact-checking claims, verifying summaries and reviews where fabrication must be avoided (lowest hallucination rate).
